# GameNatives — Native Verification Manifest

Every native in the Level 1 demo scope was checked by driving Binary Ninja MCP (`bn_function_info`) against the retail `igi.exe`, then joined to `IGINatives_VERIFIED.json`.

**Scope:** 3,050 unique natives (3,567 rows across steps, since a native may serve several steps).
**Result: 3,035 unique name-verified, 15 name conflicts, 0 lookup errors.**

Status key: `VERIFIED` = `bn_function_info` at that address returns this exact name; `CONFLICT` = the catalog label does not match the binary (use the address, not the label); `GATE-FAIL` = the catalog's own CFG gate did not pass.

> **Filename caveat:** the `<addr>-<name>.cpp` file names in this tree are auto-generated and several are **stale/wrong** (e.g. `sys/004B3100-Sys_FreeMemory_11.cpp` actually holds `Vec3_Normalize`). The **address prefix is the key**; the canonical name is the catalog/BN name in the table, not the file name.
